CORPORATE PROFILE
 四環醫藥控股集團有限公司（「四環醫藥」或「本          Founded in 2001 and listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong
公司」，連同其附屬公司為「本集團」）    （股份代號：     Limited in 2010, Sihuan Pharmaceutical Holdings Group Ltd. (“Sihuan Pharmaceutical”
 00460.HK）創立於二零零一年，二零一零年於        or the “Company”, together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”) (Stock Code:
 香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市，是一家以            00460.HK) is an international medical aesthetic and biopharmaceutical company
 創新為引領，堅持創新驅動，擁有獨立領先的            led and driven by innovation, with a leading independent production, and research
 自主生產、研究與開發（「研發」）技術平台，具          and development (“R&D”) technology platform, a rich global product pipeline and a
 備豐富的全球化產品管線和成熟卓越銷售體系            mature and excellent sales system. Focusing on high-growth therapeutic areas such as
 的國際化醫美及生物製藥企業。四環醫藥聚焦            medical aesthetics, oncology, metabolism, diabetes, cardiovascular and cerebrovascular,
 醫 美、腫 瘤、代 謝、糖 尿 病、心 腦 血 管、現 代   modern Chinese medicine and industrial hemp, it adheres to its overall strategic
 中藥及工業大麻等高增長治療領域，一直秉承            objective of “Adhering to the full-speed promotion of a two-wheeled strategy of Sihuan
「堅持全速推進四環醫美及生物製藥雙輪驅動             medical aesthetics and biopharmaceuticals” to build a leading medical aesthetics and
 戰略」的整體戰略目標來打造中國領先的醫美            biopharmaceutical enterprise in China.
 及生物製藥企業。

管理層討論及分析
MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS


二零二五年上半年，中國醫藥行業在政策深化                       In the first half of 2025, China’s pharmaceutical industry continued to deepen its
與市場結構調整的雙重驅動下，持續深化轉型                       transformation and upgrade progress, driven by both policy enhancement and market
升級進程。國家醫保談判常態化和帶量採購覆                       structural adjustments. The normalisation of national medical insurance negotiations
蓋 範 圍 的 持 續 擴 大，特 別 是 集 採 規 則 向「質 價        and the ongoing expansion of the volume-based procurement coverage, particularly
平 衡」的 優 化 演 進，正 有 力 推 動 行 業 加 速 向 創        the optimisation of centralized procurement rules towards “quality-price balance”,
新 驅 動 和 高 質 量 供 給 方 向 邁 進。伴 隨「健 康 中        have been propelling the industry towards innovation-driven and high-quality supply
國 2030」戰略的深入實施，創新藥審批效率顯                    directions. With the in-depth implementation of the “Healthy China 2030” strategy,
著提升，醫保支付更加強調臨床價值導向，為                       the efficiency of innovative drug approvals has significantly improved, while medical
擁有自主研發能力的製藥企業開闢了廣闊的發                       insurance payments have increasingly emphasised a clinical value-oriented approach,
展空間。行業分化格局愈加清晰：創新藥領域，                      creating vast development opportunities for pharmaceutical companies with independent
尤其是生物藥、腫瘤及慢性病治療賽道，國產                       R&D capabilities. As the industry has experienced distinct differentiation in the field
創新藥迎來密集商業化爆發期，本土企業積極                       of innovative drugs, particularly in biopharmaceuticals, oncology, and chronic disease
通過對外授權加速國際化步伐；而仿製藥領域                       treatments, domestic innovative drugs entered a phase of intensive commercialisation.
經歷多輪集採洗禮後，價格體系趨於穩定，具                       Local companies strived to accelerate their internationalisation through licensing out.
備卓越成本管控與規模效應的企業開始顯現觸                       Meanwhile, the pricing system in the generic drug field has stabilised after several
底企穩的積極信號。                                  rounds of centralised procurements, where companies with excellent cost control and
                                           scale effects began to show positive signs of bottoming out.


與醫藥板塊的轉型相呼應，根據行業權威報告                       In tandem with the transformation of the pharmaceutical segment, according to
數據顯示，二零二五年，中國醫美行業整體將                       authoritative industry reports, the overall medical aesthetics industry in China is expected
保持約 10% 的年複合增長率。這一強勁增長源                    to maintain a compound annual growth rate (CAGR) of approximately 10% in 2025.
於多重動力：消費升級背景下人均可支配收入                       This robust growth was derived from multiple factors: the increase in disposable income
的提升與「顏值經濟」的深化共同驅動需求釋放，                     per capita amid consumption upgrades and the deepening of the “aesthetic economy”
輕醫美消費加速向二三線城市滲透；監管部門                       jointly drove the release of demand that non-invasive medical aesthetics consumption
對水貨及非法產品的持續嚴厲打擊，顯著加速                       rapidly penetrated into second- and third-tier cities; the regulatory authorities’
了合規產品對灰色市場的替代進程；以膠原蛋                       ongoing crackdown on grey market and illegal products has significantly accelerated
白、再生材料為代表的新技術以及高端注射器                       the replacement process of compliant products in the grey market; new technologies
械的創新迭代，有效帶動了客單價與客戶複購                       represented by collagen and regenerative materials, along with innovations in high-end
率的雙升。                                      injection devices, have effectively driven an increase in both average transaction value
                                           and customer repurchase rates.


在這一深刻變革的行業圖景中，成功實現戰略                       In this landscape of profound industry transformation, companies successfully achieving
轉型的企業正迎來關鍵機遇期。聚焦於醫美、                       strategic transitions are entering a critical window of opportunity. Leading enterprises
創新藥、生物藥領域的領先企業，其前瞻性佈                       focusing on medical aesthetics, innovative drugs, and biopharmaceuticals are seeing
局與持續投入開始進入收穫階段。在創新藥與                       their forward-looking strategies and sustained investments begin to yield results. In
生物藥領域，高價值研發管線正加速推進，新                       the fields of innovative drugs and biopharmaceuticals, progress of high-value R&D
產品密集進入上市和商業化階段。在醫美領域，                      pipelines are accelerated, with new products rapidly advancing to market launch
構建了完善合規產品矩陣、掌握核心生物材料                       and commercialization stages. In the medical aesthetics sector, companies that have
技 術、並 擁 有 深 度 渠 道 覆 蓋 的 企 業，其「自 主          built comprehensive and compliant product portfolios, mastered core biomaterial
研發 + 全球引進」模式持續產出滿足市場升級需                    technologies, and established wide channel coverage are leveraging a “self-developed
求的產品，將佔據有利地位。                              + global sourcing” model to continuously deliver products that meet evolving market
                                           demands, securing a competitive edge.


綜上可見，二零二五年上半年的中國醫藥健康                       In summary, the first half of 2025 marked a pivotal stage for China’s pharmaceutical
產業，正處於創新驅動、質量提升和結構優化                       and healthcare industry, driven by innovation, quality enhancement, and structural
的關鍵階段。政策引導、市場需求與技術變革                       optimization. Policy guidance, market demand, and technological advancements
共同塑造了新的競爭格局。在此背景下，四環                       are collectively shaping a new competitive landscape. Against this backdrop, Sihuan
醫藥，一個以創新研發為核心驅動力的領先醫                       Pharmaceutical, a leading medical aesthetics and biopharmaceutical company driven
美及生物製藥企業，通過前瞻的戰略聚焦、持                       by innovative R&D, has established significant competitive advantages in both fields
續的高強度研發投入、以及強大的商業化體系，                      through its forward-looking strategic focus, sustained high-intensity R&D investment,
已在醫美與生物製藥領域確立了顯著的競爭優                       and robust commercialization system, laying a solid foundation for seizing future growth
勢，為把握未來增長機遇奠定了堅實基礎。                        opportunities.

品項。本集團旗下已成功獲批上市的「少                approved products, including the “PCL filler” Qingyan, and the “PLLA filler”

臻 ®，均屬於再生產品類別中技術成熟、               characterized by mature technology, clear efficacy, and widespread consumer
效果明確且獲得廣泛消費者認可的代表                 recognition. Management believes that the launch of these two blockbuster

MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS


人力資源及僱員薪金                                  Human resources and remuneration of employees
人力資源是本集團在充滿挑戰的環境中得以成                       Human resources are indispensable assets to the Group’s success in a competitive
功的不可或缺資產。本集團致力為全體僱員提                       environment. The Group is committed to providing competitive remuneration packages
供具競爭力的薪酬待遇，定期檢討人力資源政                       to all the employees and regularly reviewing human resources policies, to encourage
策，以鼓勵僱員努力提升本公司價值及促進本                       employees to work towards enhancing the value of the Company and promoting the
公司的可持續增長。本集團亦已採納購股權計                       sustainable growth of the Company. The Group has also adopted share option scheme
劃及股份獎勵計劃，以表揚及獎勵員工對本集                       and share award scheme to recognise and reward the contribution of the employees for
團之營運及未來發展作出的貢獻。本集團持續                       the benefit of the Group’s operations and future development. The Group continues
推動人才培養與發展體系建設，圍繞不同層級                       to promote the building of talent training and development system, and conducts
崗位任職能力標準展開在線與線下的培訓工作，                      online and offline training based on the competency standards for positions at different
促進本集團人才的培育與發展，保障各類人才                       levels to promote the cultivation and development of talents in the Group and ensure
的持續供給。                                     continuous supply of various types of talents.

會擁有酌情權可在授出購股權時設定（其中包括）                     consider that allowing the Board discretion to fix, among other things, the minimum

效目標及認購價，將可更有效地達成購股權計                       and the subscription price, upon the grant of options will better serve the purpose of
劃的目的，原因是這可讓董事會應承授人的具                       the Share Option Scheme as this will allow the Board to grant options that cater to the
體情況，經考慮承授人的資歷、經驗、過往工                       specific circumstances of the Grantee, taking into consideration the Grantee’s seniority,
作表現、專業領域等因素後授出購股權，因而                       experience, past work performance, field of expertise, etc., and thereby providing
可給予承授人適當的鼓勵及激勵。                            appropriate motivation and incentive to the Grantee.

二零二二年股份獎勵計劃的目的                    Purpose of the 2022 Share Award Scheme
二零二二年股份獎勵計劃為一項股份激勵計劃，             The 2022 Share Award Scheme is a share incentive scheme and is established to
乃為認可及表彰承授人對本集團所作出或可能              recognize and acknowledge the contributions which the grantees have made or may
作出的貢獻而設立。二零二二年股份獎勵計劃              make to the Group. The 2022 Share Award Scheme will provide the grantees with
將向承授人提供個人持有本公司股權的機會，              the opportunity to own a personal stake in the Company with a view to achieving the
以達至以下目標：(i) 激勵承授人；及╱或 (ii) 吸      following objectives: (i) motivating the grantees; and/or (ii) attracting and retaining or
引及挽留所作貢獻有利、將有利或可能有利於              otherwise maintaining on-going relationship with the grantees whose contributions are,
本集團長期發展的承授人，或以其他方式與彼              will be or are likely to be beneficial to the long-term growth of the Group.
等維持持續關係。

閱中期財務資料包括主要向負責財務和會計事                     Standards Board. A review of interim financial information consists of making inquiries,
務的人員作出詢問，及進行分析性和其他審閱                     primarily of persons responsible for financial and accounting matters, and applying
程序。審閱範圍遠少於根據國際審計準則進行                     analytical and other review procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than
審核的範圍，故我們無法保證我們將知悉在審                     an audit conducted in accordance with International Standards on Auditing and
核中可能被發現的所有重大事項。因此，我們                     consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that we would become aware of all

1.   公 司 及 集 團資料                               1.   CORPORATE AND GROUP INFORMATION
     四環醫藥控股集團有限公司（「本公司」）                            Sihuan Pharmaceutical Holdings Group Ltd. (the “Company”) is incorporated in
     根據百慕達公司法於百慕達註冊成立為                              Bermuda under the Bermuda Companies Act as an exempted company.
     獲豁免公司。


      本 公 司 為 投 資 控 股 公 司。本 公 司 及 其 附               The Company is an investment holding company. The principal activities of the
      屬公司（統稱「本集團」）的主要業務為於                           Company and its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”) are the research and
      中華人民共和國（「中國」）研究及開發                            development (“R&D”), manufacture and sale of pharmaceutical and medical
     （「研發」）、製造及銷售醫藥及醫美產品。                           aesthetic products in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”).


     本 公 司 註 冊 辦 事 處 地 址 為 Clarendon                The address of the Company’s registered office is Clarendon House, 2 Church
     House, 2 Church Street, Hamilton HM 11,        Street, Hamilton HM 11, Bermuda. The address of the principal place of business
     Bermuda。本 集 團 香 港 主 要 營 業 地 點 為                of the Group in Hong Kong is Room 4905, Office Tower, Convention Plaza, 1
     香港灣仔港灣道1號會展廣場辦公大樓                              Harbour Road, Wanchai, Hong Kong, and the address of the principal place of
     4905 室，及北京主要營業地點為中國北                           business in Beijing is 22/F, Building 4, Zhubang 2000, West Balizhuang, Chaoyang
     京 市 朝 陽 區 八 里 莊 西 里 住 邦 2000，4 號               District, Beijing 100025, the PRC.
     樓 22 層（郵編：100025）。

                                                    ACCOUNTING POLICIES
     2.1 編製基準                                       2.1 Basis of preparation
          截至二零二五年六月三十日止六                                  The interim condensed consolidated financial information for the six
          個月的中期簡明綜合財務資料乃                                  months ended 30 June 2025 has been prepared in accordance with IAS 34

          務報告 編製。中期簡明綜合財務資                                information does not include all the information and disclosures required
          料不包括年度財務報表中規定的                                  in the annual financial statements, and should be read in conjunction with
          所有資料及披露且應與本集團截                                  the Group’s annual consolidated financial statements for the year ended 31

          年度的年度綜合財務報表一併閱讀。

               實體應如何評估某種貨幣是否可                            currency is exchangeable into another currency and how it shall estimate a
               兌換為另一種貨幣，以及在缺乏可                           spot exchange rate at a measurement date when exchangeability is lacking.
               兌換性的情況下，實體應如何估計                           The amendments require disclosures of information that enable users of
               於計量日期的即期匯率。該等修訂                           financial statements to understand the impact of a currency not being
               要求披露讓財務報表使用者能夠                            exchangeable. As the currencies that the Group had transacted with and
               了解貨幣不可兌換的影響的資料。                           the functional currencies of group entities for translation into the Group’s
               由於本集團交易之貨幣及集團實                            presentation currency were exchangeable, the amendments did not have
               體換算為本集團呈列貨幣之功能                            any impact on the interim condensed consolidated financial information.
               貨幣均可兌換，故該等修訂預期不
               會對本集團的中期簡明綜合財務
               資料產生任何影響。


3.    分部資料                                    3.   SEGMENT INFORMATION
      就 管 理 而 言，本 集 團 基 於 其 產 品 及 服 務              For management purposes, the Group is organised into business units based
      劃分業務單位，三個呈報業務分部如下：                           on their products and services and has three reportable operating segments as
                                                   follows:


      (a)      醫美產品分部包括填充類、塑形類、                    (a)   the medical aesthetic products segment including filling, shaping,
               支撐類、補充類、光電設備類、體                           supporting, supplementing, optoelectronic devices, body sculpturing, skin
               雕類、皮膚管理類及其他以及提供                           care and others to provide non- or minimally invasive medical aesthetics
               無創或微創醫美綜合解決方案；                            comprehensive solutions;

      (b)      創新藥及其他藥品分部；及                        (b)   the innovative medicine and other medicine segment; and


      (c)      仿製藥分部。                              (c)   the generic medicine segment.


      管理層獨立監察本集團經營分部的業績，                           Management monitors the results of the Group’s operating segments separately
      以作出有關資源分配及表現評估的決策。                           for the purpose of making decisions about resource allocation and performance
      分部表現乃根據可報告分部溢利╱虧損                            assessment. Segment performance is evaluated based on reportable segment
     （其為經調整除稅前溢利╱虧損的計量）                            profit/loss, which is a measure of adjusted profit/loss before tax. The adjusted
      予 以 評 估。經 調 整 除 稅 前 溢 利 ╱ 虧 損 的              profit/loss before tax is measured consistently with the Group’s profit/loss before
      計量與本集團之除稅前溢利╱虧損的計                            tax except that interest income, non-lease-related finance costs, dividend income,
      量 一 致，惟 利 息 收 入、非 租 賃 相 關 融 資                fair value gains/losses from the Group’s financial instruments as well as head office
      成 本、股 息 收 入、本 集 團 金 融 工 具 的 公                and corporate expenses are excluded from such measurement.
      平值收益╱虧損連同總部及公司開支不
      計入該計量內。

21. 金 融 工 具 的公平值及公平                          21. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY OF
    值 層 級（續）                                     FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (continued)
     按公平值計入損益的非上市股權投資的                              The fair values of unlisted equity investments designated at fair value through
     公 平 值，乃 根 據 並 非 由 可 見 市 價 或 比 率                profit or loss have been estimated using a market-based valuation technique
     支 持 之 假 設，使 用 市 場 估 值 方 法 預 測。                 based on assumptions that are not supported by observable market prices or rates.
     估 值 要 求 董 事 根 據 行 業、規 模、槓 桿 及                  The valuation requires the directors to determine comparable public companies
     戰 略 釐 定 可 資 比 較 公 眾 公 司，並 就 識 別                based on industry, size, leverage and strategy, and to calculate an appropriate
     出的各可資比較公司計算合適價格倍數，                             price multiple, such as enterprise value to earnings before interest, taxes,
     例 如 企 業 價 值 對 除 利 息、稅 項、折 舊 及                  depreciation and amortisation (“EV/EBITDA”) multiple and price to earnings (“P/E”)
     攤銷前盈利（「EV/EBITDA」）倍數及價格對                       multiple, for each comparable company identified. The multiple is calculated by
     盈利（「P/E」）倍數。倍數乃以可資比較公                          dividing the enterprise value of the comparable company by an earnings measure.
     司的企業價值除以盈利衡量計算得出。                              The trading multiple is then discounted for considerations such as illiquidity and
     交易倍數以可資比較公司根據公司獨有                              size differences between the comparable companies based on company-specific
     因 素 及 情 況 的 考 慮 因 素，例 如 非 流 通 性                facts and circumstances. The discounted multiple is applied to the corresponding
     及 規 模 差 異，予 以 貼 現。貼 現 倍 數 用 於                  earnings measure of the unlisted equity investments to measure the fair value.
     非上市股權投資的相應盈利衡量以計量                              The directors believe that the estimated fair values resulting from the valuation
     公 平 值。董 事 認 為 估 值 方 法 得 出 的 估 計                technique, which are recorded in the consolidated statement of financial position,
     公平值（記錄於綜合財務狀況表）及相關                             and the related changes in fair values, which are recorded in the consolidated
     公平值變動（記錄於綜合損益及其他全面                             statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income, are reasonable, and
     收益表）屬合理，並為報告期末最合適的                             that they were the most appropriate values at the end of the reporting period.
     價值。

     本 集 團 投 資 於 非 上 市 投 資，即 中 國 內 地                The Group invests in unlisted investments, which represent wealth management
     銀 行 發 行 的 理 財 產 品。本 集 團 已 使 用 貼                products issued by banks in Mainland China. The Group has estimated the fair
     現 現 金 流 量 估 值 模 型，根 據 類 似 年 期 及                value of these unlisted investments by using a discounted cash flow valuation
     風險的工具的市場利率估算該等非上市                              model based on the market interest rates of instruments with similar terms and
     投資的公平值。                                        risks.

     對於按公平值計入其他全面收益的非上                              For the fair value of the unlisted equity investments at fair value through other
     市 股 權 投 資 的 公 平 值，管 理 層 已 估 計 使                comprehensive income, management has estimated the potential effect of using
     用合理可行的替代方法作為估值模型參                              reasonably possible alternatives as inputs to the valuation model.
     數的潛在影響。

     下文載列於二零二五年六月三十日及二                              Set out below is a summary of valuation technique and significant unobservable
     零二四年十二月三十一日非上市權益投                              input to the valuation of unlisted equity investments together with a quantitative
     資估值的估值方法及重大不可觀察參數                              sensitivity analysis as at 30 June 2025 and 31 December 2024:
     以及定量敏感度分析概要：
